# IMPERIA COLD WAR — India's 1970 programmes.
# COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_SECTOR_MANDATES_AGGREGATED_V1
# COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_PARTY_MANDATES_AGGREGATED_V1
COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_initialise = {
 if = {
  limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_initialised } }
 if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = COLDWAR_RAJ_PEOPLE_congress_leader } } set_variable = { COLDWAR_RAJ_PEOPLE_congress_leader = 1 } }
 add_ideas = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_licence_raj
 add_ideas = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_food_insecurity
 add_ideas = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_poverty
 add_ideas = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_centre_state
 add_ideas = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_defence_constraint
 set_country_flag = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_initialised
 }
}
COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_reduce_food_insecurity = {
 if = { limit = { has_idea = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_food_insecurity } swap_ideas = { remove_idea = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_food_insecurity add_idea = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_food_insecurity_reduced } }
}
COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_reduce_licence_raj = {
 if = { limit = { has_idea = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_licence_raj } swap_ideas = { remove_idea = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_licence_raj add_idea = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_licence_raj_reduced } }
}
COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_reduce_centre_state_tensions = {
 if = { limit = { has_idea = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_centre_state } swap_ideas = { remove_idea = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_centre_state add_idea = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_centre_state_reduced } }
}
COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_reduce_defence_constraint = {
 if = { limit = { has_idea = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_defence_constraint } swap_ideas = { remove_idea = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_defence_constraint add_idea = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_defence_constraint_reduced } }
}
COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_check_defence_modernisation = {
 if = { limit = { OR = { has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_army_coordinated_programme has_country_flag = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_army_coordinated_programme } OR = { has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_defence_production_coordinated_programme has_country_flag = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_defence_production_coordinated_programme } has_idea = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_defence_constraint_reduced }
  remove_ideas = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_defence_constraint_reduced
 }
}
COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_resolve_food_insecurity = {
 if = { limit = { has_idea = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_food_insecurity } remove_ideas = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_food_insecurity }
 if = { limit = { has_idea = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_food_insecurity_reduced } remove_ideas = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_food_insecurity_reduced }
 COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_check_poverty_relief = yes
}
COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_resolve_licence_raj = {
 if = { limit = { has_idea = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_licence_raj } remove_ideas = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_licence_raj }
 if = { limit = { has_idea = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_licence_raj_reduced } remove_ideas = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_licence_raj_reduced }
}
COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_resolve_centre_state_tensions = {
 if = { limit = { has_idea = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_centre_state } remove_ideas = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_centre_state }
 if = { limit = { has_idea = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_centre_state_reduced } remove_ideas = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_centre_state_reduced }
}
COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_check_poverty_relief = {
 if = { limit = { has_idea = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_poverty NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_food_insecurity } NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_food_insecurity_reduced } OR = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_social_reform_complete has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_society_final_a has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_society_final_b } }
  swap_ideas = { remove_idea = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_poverty add_idea = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_poverty_reduced }
 }
}
# COLDWAR | India 1970 focus completion rewards. Generated from the approved manifest.
COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_inc_mandate_complete = {
 hidden_effect = {
  if = { limit = { check_variable = { ruling_party = 3 } } set_country_flag = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_inc_mandate_owner_inc }
 }
 custom_effect_tooltip = { localization_key = modifies_dynamic_modifier_tt MODIFIER = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_inc_aggregate_mandate }
 effect_tooltip = {
  add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_inc_mandate_stability_factor = 0.05 tooltip = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_mandate_change_stability_factor_0_tt }
 }

 hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_inc_mandate_completing COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_sync_party_mandates = yes clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_inc_mandate_completing set_country_flag = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_party_mandates_dirty }
}
COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_inc_a_entry_complete = {
 hidden_effect = { set_country_flag = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_inc_a_entry }
 effect_tooltip = {
  add_stability = 0.02
 }
 hidden_effect = {
  if = { limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_inc_a_entry_one_time_granted } }
   add_stability = 0.02
   set_country_flag = COLDWAR_RAJ_1970_inc_a_entry_one_time_granted
  }
 }
}
